Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The enzymes operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 101. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 546 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the enzymes space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 101 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 546. QED.
